Opens with a full explanation of the whole repo (architecture, entry points, how the pieces fit), then runs a four-stage loop: Learn (first principles, analogies) → Check understanding (one question at a time, adaptive difficulty) → Practical application (real examples) → Feynman (learner explains back, gaps identified). Teaches, never modifies. ([ruokai-lai/learn-repo](https://github.com/ruokai-lai/learn-repo/tree/HEAD/skills/learn-repo))
- **learn-repo**: Ingest a code repo into this Obsidian vault as a structured, source-verified set of notes (a MOC + overview(s) + one component note per module) so teammates and their AI can onboard from the vault instead of re-reading the repo. Checkpoint-driven, two human gates. Use when asked to "learn"/"ingest"/"document a repo into the vault". Invoke as /learn-repo &lt;repo-path-or-name&gt; [target-folder-name]. ([thenomadops-org/agentic-harness](https://github.com/thenomadops-org/agentic-harness/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/learn-repo))
- **learn-repo**: Turns a codebase into a hands-on course: maps its structure, picks a reading order, writes guided tours of the key files grounded in real line references, and sets exercises such as "trace this request end to end", "predict what this test does", and "add a small feature", each with escalating hints. Saves everything under learn/&lt;repo-slug&gt;/. Use when the user says "help me learn this codebase", "onboard me to this repo", "teach me how this project works", "I just joined, where do I start", "make a course out of this repo", or "give me exercises on this code". ([vivekbhange-hub/learn-anything-skill](https://github.com/vivekbhange-hub/learn-anything-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/learn-repo))

- **learning-deck**: 针对一个主题、一个方法或一篇文章里的核心概念，先用三轮访谈摸清用户已经站在哪一层，再只补他缺的那几层，产出一份图文并茂、可离线打开的 HTML 自学报告：知识地图、每个知识点一节（一句话定义 → 它解决什么问题 → 机制示意图 → 常见误解与边界 → 出处）、方法对比表、术语对照和精读清单。用户说想搞懂某篇文章里的某个概念、某个方法到底是怎么回事、这个领域的基础知识是什么、要扫盲、要入门、想从零开始学、给我讲讲 X、X 是什么原理、这东西为什么这么设计，都用这个 skill。它比直接讲解多做的事：先问清起点再决定讲什么，检索的是综述、tutorial 与奠基论文而不是最新进展，每篇回源核验并下载全文，机制图看懂原文后重画成内联 SVG。给的是一篇文章时，它讲的是**文章里的概念**；讲文章本身怎么写进你的论文是 `/paper-summary` 的活。 ([zhangyee/kydog](https://github.com/zhangyee/kydog/tree/HEAD/src/skills/learning-deck) · ★2)
